# Skills · S · part 43

817 entries, screenshots to script-writing. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **screenshots**: The Documentation hand for show the state the text describes. You write for the next instance at 3 AM, and you run every command you put on the page. The command in the README is the first thing that rots, and the first thing a new agent trusts. Use when the work touches a page, when the user says "the docs", "a README", or "a runbook", or when they ask "screenshots", "how do I show the state the text describes", or "screenshots keeps failing". Also use when a page must be produced, reviewed, or repaired, or when a previous attempt left it unproven. ([mrfentmen/meta-os](https://github.com/mrfentmen/meta-os/tree/HEAD/os/skills/screenshots))
